# Skills · A · part 104

691 entries, alphagenome-atlas-website-links to alterlab-pylabrobot. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **alphagenome-atlas-website-links**: Constructs deep-links and URLs for the AlphaGenome Atlas website. Supports generating single-variant exploration links (1-based chr:pos:ref&gt;alt), genomic locus views (1-based closed chr:start-end), candidate summary tables, and AlphaGenome reference vs. alternate predictions. Use whenever visualizing, exploring, charting, or linking genetic variants and genomic loci on the AlphaGenome Atlas, or when asked to inspect, view, or link predictions for a genomic variant. ([FridrichMethod/awesome-skills](https://github.com/FridrichMethod/awesome-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/alphagenome_atlas_website_links) · ★17 · 11 copies)
- **alphagenome-avi-interpretation**: Comprehensive interpretation framework for AlphaGenome Variant Impact (AVI) scores across 18 modalities, Phred scale tiers, and Atlas links. ([GoogleCloudPlatform/LifeSciences](https://github.com/GoogleCloudPlatform/LifeSciences/tree/HEAD/applications/pharma-on-gemini-enterprise/variant-annotation-agent/app/skills/alphagenome-avi-interpretation) · ★58)
- **alphagenome-finetuning**: Fine-tune or transfer-learn AlphaGenome-PyTorch on custom genomic data — pick a mode (linear probe, LoRA, Locon, full), train on BigWig tracks with `agt finetune`, use adapters, delta checkpoints, multi-GPU/sequence parallelism, or the Python transfer API. Use when ADAPTING/TRAINING the model on new data, not when running predictions with the pretrained model. ([genomicsxai/alphagenome-pytorch](https://github.com/genomicsxai/alphagenome-pytorch/tree/HEAD/plugins/alphagenome/skills/alphagenome-finetuning) · ★165 · 2 copies)
- **alphagenome-predictions**: Run AlphaGenome-PyTorch to get genomic track predictions — via the `agt predict` CLI (single locus, BED regions, whole chromosomes, raw FASTA sequences, or per-gene count tables/AnnData), variant effect scoring (`agt score`), or the Python API. Covers picking a specific assay, cell type, or resolution, e.g. "get DNase predictions from GM12878 at 128bp", "write a wrapper for all K562 predictions", filtering tracks by metadata (biosample, assay, ontology, strand). Use when the task is about USING the model for inference/predictions, not developing the package. ([genomicsxai/alphagenome-pytorch](https://github.com/genomicsxai/alphagenome-pytorch/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/alphagenome-predictions) · ★165 · 2 copies)
- **alphagenome-single-variant-analysis**: Analyzes genetic variant effects on gene expression (RNA-seq), chromatin accessibility (DNASE), histone marks (ChIP), and transcription factors using the AlphaGenome API. Use when the user asks about non-coding variant effects, pathogenicity, clinical significance, disease associations, functional effects, gene expression changes, splicing disruption, or regulatory effects in promoters and enhancers. Also use for resolving biological terms to tissue/cell-type ontologies (UBERON/CL) or analyzing variants in chr:pos:ref&gt;alt format. ([google-deepmind/science-skills](https://github.com/google-deepmind/science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/alphagenome_single_variant_analysis) · ★3.5k · 54 copies)
- **alphagenome-single-variant-analysis**: Source: `science_skills/alphagenome_single_variant_analysis/` ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/alphagenome-single-variant-analysis) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **alphagenome-single-variant-analysis**: Analyze genetic variant effects with the AlphaGenome API: expression, chromatin accessibility, histone marks and TF binding; non-coding variant pathogenicity and splicing effects; resolve tissue/cell-type ontologies (UBERON/CL). ([wtbxsjy/my_skills](https://github.com/wtbxsjy/my_skills/tree/HEAD/skills/alphagenome-single-variant-analysis))
- **alphagenome-variant-analysis**: Analyze how a single non-coding genetic variant affects gene expression, chromatin accessibility, histone marks, transcription factors, and splicing using the AlphaGenome API. Use when the user gives a variant in chr:pos:ref/alt form and asks about regulatory or splicing effects, tissue-specific impact, or pathogenicity of a non-coding change. Requires an AlphaGenome API key. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/alphagenome-variant-analysis) · ★4)
- **alphagenome-variant-effects**: Use Google DeepMind AlphaGenome to predict tissue-aware regulatory effects of DNA sequence variants across expression, splicing, chromatin, and contact-map outputs. Use when prioritizing noncoding variants, comparing reference and alternate alleles, visualizing predicted regulatory changes, or designing focused AlphaGenome API analyses. ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/alphagenome-variant-effects) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **alphagenome-variant-impact-score**: Score, annotate, and analyze the functional impact of genetic variants using AlphaGenome Variant Impact (AVI) scores. Query variants in chr:pos:ref&gt;alt format, annotate VCF/tabular callsets, perform saturation mutagenesis window scans (1-based closed chr:start-end), and extract GENCODE v46 GTF gene/exon/junction coordinates all via the AlphaGenome Atlas API. ([google-deepmind/science-skills](https://github.com/google-deepmind/science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/alphagenome_variant_impact_score) · ★3.5k · 30 copies)
